/></button><div class="_8xfq hidden_elem" id="u_0_2a_wp"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""> Reels isn’t currently available to everyone. Some accounts may be able to see reels, but won&#039;t be able to record them.</p><br /><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can record and edit videos up to 20 minutes with Instagram Reels. You can add effects and music to your reel or use your own original audio.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""></p><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Record your reel</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><p class="_3p8"> Click <img class="_254 img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=instagram-plusaddpost&amp;density=1" alt="create new" /><b class="_9o-0"> Create</b> on the left. </p></p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Click <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=ig-post-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Post</b>.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Click <b>Select from computer</b> and choose the content you want to share from your device. Click <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=ig-media-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Media</b> in the bottom right, then click <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=ig-addmoreplus-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Add</b> to add another file.</p></li><li>Click <b>Next</b> in the top right. From here you can:<ul class="uiList _4of _4kg"><li><div class="fcb">Use the slider on the right below <b>Cover photo</b> to choose a cover photo from the clip or click <b>Select from computer</b> to choose one on your device.</div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Use the slider on the right below <b>Trim</b> to trim the beginning or end of your clip.</p></div></li></ul></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Next</b> in the top right. Here you can write a caption, add a location, tag people, add alternate text, and adjust your post&#039;s settings.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Share</b> in the top right.</p></li></ol><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If you share your reel to Explore, it can also be seen on the Reels section of your profile.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""></p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>How long your reel can be</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can record one or multiple clips that add up to 20 minutes. The progress bar at the top shows how long you’ve recorded.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Note: </b>Reels over 3 minutes won’t be recommended to new audiences.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""></p><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Learn more about <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/639718330257952" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2b_s9"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">saving and editing a draft of your reel</span></span></a>.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Learn from creators on how to create engaging Reels <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://creators.instagram.com/create-engaging-content" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2c_x7"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">here</span></span></a>.</p></div></div></div></div><div style="border-color:black" class="_8xfk _8xfl"><button data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-2&quot;&#125;" class="_8xfm" id="u_0_2d_zn"><div class="_8xfn"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs2" style="">Find reels to watch on Instagram</p></div></div></div><img class="_9csc img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-2-expand&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yM/r/LzRvQbnX_o1.svg" alt="" id="u_0_2e_9w" /><img class="_9csb hidden_elem img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-2-collapse&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yA/r/CwHyQHI-5OE.svg" alt="" id="u_0_2f_0g" /></button><div class="_8xfq hidden_elem" id="u_0_2g_0G"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">To find reels to watch on Instagram</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Go to <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://www.instagram.com/" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2h_wV"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw"> instagram.com</span></span></a>.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Click <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=ig-reels-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Reels</b> on the left.</p></li></ol><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Learn <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/505299634436870" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2i_Ek"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">how to only see posts for people you follow</span></span></a> in your Instagram feed.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Visit the <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://transparency.fb.com/features/explaining-ranking/ig-reels-chaining/?referrer=2" target="_blank" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2j_zz"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">Meta Transparency Center</span></span></a> for information on how an artificial intelligence (AI) system selects, ranks and delivers the content you see on Instagram Reels.</p></div></div></div></div><div style="border-color:black" class="_8xfk _8xfl"><button data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-3&quot;&#125;" class="_8xfm" id="u_0_2k_qZ"><div class="_8xfn"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs2" style="">How do I create a reel using a template on Instagram?</p></div></div></div><img class="_9csc img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-3-expand&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yM/r/LzRvQbnX_o1.svg" alt="" id="u_0_2l_+m" /><img class="_9csb hidden_elem img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-3-collapse&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yA/r/CwHyQHI-5OE.svg" alt="" id="u_0_2m_hn" /></button><div class="_8xfq hidden_elem" id="u_0_2n_Yp"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Templates are only available for reels that include music and at least 3 clips.</p><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can create your own version of an Instagram reel by using a template. Templates allow you to use the same music and timing as the original reel, and replace the photos or video clips with your own. </p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can find templates and save your favorite templates in the Template Browser.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Create a reel with a template</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=instagram-plus-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Create</b> in the top left or swipe right anywhere in Feed.<br /></p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Reel</b> at the bottom.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=instagram-templates&amp;density=1" alt="Templates" /><b> Templates</b>.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Swipe through to <b>Browse</b> the available templates and tap one you’d like to use.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Add media</b>, then tap the photos or videos in the order you’d like them to appear.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Next</b>.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If you need to change any of your clips, tap the clip and then tap <b>Replace media</b>. Tap the photo or video you’d like to use instead.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Next</b> to <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/2720958398006062" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2o_dV"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">preview and share your reel</span></span></a>.</p></li></ol><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can also use a reel you see as a template by tapping <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=instagram-templates-filled&amp;density=1" alt="Templates" /><b> Template by...</b> then tap <b>Use Template</b>. Not all reels can be used as a template.</p></div></div></div></div><div style="border-color:black" class="_8xfk _8xfl"><button data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-4&quot;&#125;" class="_8xfm" id="u_0_2p_gq"><div class="_8xfn"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs2" style="">What audio you can use in your reel on Instagram</p></div></div></div><img class="_9csc img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-4-expand&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yM/r/LzRvQbnX_o1.svg" alt="" id="u_0_2q_ug" /><img class="_9csb hidden_elem img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-4-collapse&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yA/r/CwHyQHI-5OE.svg" alt="" id="u_0_2r_sr" /></button><div class="_8xfq hidden_elem" id="u_0_2s_N1"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can record a reel with your own original audio, with original audio by another Reels creator, or with audio from the Instagram audio library <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/664508917489819" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2t_AD"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">using the Audio tool</span></span></a> in Reels. Note that you can’t use original audio from a feed video unless you remix the video.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can also learn how to <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/825171856327681" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2u_3C"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">replace a muted audio track on your Instagram reel</span></span></a>.</p><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Original audio</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Original audio is unique audio you created, either while filming your reel in the Instagram app or in a video you uploaded, or unique audio by another Reels creator. When someone posts a reel with original audio, it will display their username.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">When you post a reel on a public account, anyone on Instagram, the Meta AI apps or Meta AI websites can record a reel using your original audio. You can turn this off for the Meta AI apps or websites at any time in your Instagram settings. Note: If you cross-post a public reel to Facebook, anyone on that app can create a reel with your original audio unless you’ve turned this off in your Facebook settings. Additionally, anyone on Instagram can record a reel using your original audio and crosspost it to their Facebook profile or Page.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">To allow or disallow people on Meta AI apps or websites to create reels using your original audio:</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=fb-hamburgerlight-iphone&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Menu</b> or your profile picture in the bottom right to go to your profile.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=fb-settings-iphone&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Settings &amp; privacy</b>, then tap <b>Settings</b>.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Under <b>How others can interact with you</b>, tap <b>Sharing and reuse</b>.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Under <b>Allow people to create with and reuse your original audio on Meta AI</b>, switch the toggle on or off next to <b>Reels</b>.</p></li></ol><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If you upload licensed audio as original audio, we may change the description to the artist&#039;s name and song.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Licensed audio</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Licensed audio is copyrighted audio available on the Instagram audio library and includes things like music, movies, TV shows and sound effects. All licensed audio must be attributed or linked to the artist and include the name of the track.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Keep in mind that as we make sure to uphold our agreements with music rights holders, audio tracks in your posts can become muted as a result of any changes made to these agreements. While the audio tracks of your post may be muted, <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/1392551434468488" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2v_dI"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">your post can still be viewed</span></span></a>.</p><div class="mvm uiP fsm"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Notification that your reel was added to an audio page</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">When we detect the audio you recorded or uploaded uses licensed audio, we’ll automatically change attribution from Original Audio to the artist and song title. Your reel will be added to the <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="http://help.instagram.com/666412317561393" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_2w_tL"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">audio page for that song</span></span></a>, where more people on Instagram may find it.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>What if the attribution is wrong?</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If we’ve identified the wrong audio track, you can remove your reel from the audio page.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">On Android, tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=ig-three-dots-more-vertical&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Options</b> at the bottom of the reel, then tap <b>Remove From Audio Page</b>.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">On iPhone, tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=ig-three-dots-more-horizontal&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Options</b> at the bottom of the reel, then tap <b>Remove From Audio Page</b>. </p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Your audio will be attributed to you and will say Original Audio next to your username.</p></div></div></div></div><div style="border-color:black" class="_8xfk _8xfl"><button data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-5&quot;&#125;" class="_8xfm" id="u_0_2x_48"><div class="_8xfn"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs2" style="">Save and edit drafts of your reel on Instagram</p></div></div></div><img class="_9csc img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-5-expand&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yM/r/LzRvQbnX_o1.svg" alt="" id="u_0_2y_Wp" /><img class="_9csb hidden_elem img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-5-collapse&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yA/r/CwHyQHI-5OE.svg" alt="" id="u_0_2z_D2" /></button><div class="_8xfq hidden_elem" id="u_0_30_be"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><div class="mbl pam _b4p uiBoxLightblue dashedborder">Drafts are saved onto your device. You won’t be able to access a saved draft from another device, even if you log in to your account. If you uninstall the app, your drafts will be lost.</div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/2720958398006062" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_31_zD"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">record a reel</span></span></a> and save it to your Instagram account or camera roll before sharing it. Drafts will be saved until you share or delete them. There isn’t a limit to how many drafts you can save.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""></p><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Keep in mind, downloading your reel won’t save any music you added from the Instagram music library.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Save a draft of your reel to your Instagram</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Next</b> on the reel you&#039;re recording.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Next</b>, then tap <b>Save draft</b> at the bottom.</p></li></ol><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""></p><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Edit a draft of your reel from your Instagram</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><p class="_3p8">Tap <img class="_254 img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=instagram-user-profile&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b class="_9o-0"> Profile</b> or your profile picture in the bottom right to go to your profile.<br /></p></p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=ig-reels-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Reels</b> below your profile info.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Drafts</b>, then select the reel you want to edit.</p></li></ol><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""></p><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Save a draft of your reel to your camera roll</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Next</b> on the reel you&#039;re recording.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Swipe up to edit.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Swipe left to right, then tap <b>Save</b> at the bottom.</p></li></ol><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""></p><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Edit a draft of your reel from your camera roll</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=instagram-blacknewpostupload-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Create</b> at the top or swipe right from anywhere in Feed.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Scroll to <b>Reel</b> at the bottom of the screen.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap to choose the draft of the video you want to edit from your camera roll. You may tap <b>Recents</b>, then tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=facebook-watchtabplayvideo-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b>Videos</b> in the top left to filter out recent videos from your camera roll.</p></li></ol><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""></p><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can record more clips and add music, effects, stickers and text to your reel.</p></div></div></div></div><div style="border-color:black" class="_8xfk _8xfl"><button data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-6&quot;&#125;" class="_8xfm" id="u_0_32_Vz"><div class="_8xfn"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs2" style="">Share Instagram reels to Facebook</p></div></div></div><img class="_9csc img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-6-expand&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yM/r/LzRvQbnX_o1.svg" alt="" id="u_0_33_I7" /><img class="_9csb hidden_elem img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-6-collapse&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yA/r/CwHyQHI-5OE.svg" alt="" id="u_0_34_zH" /></button><div class="_8xfq hidden_elem" id="u_0_35_EC"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"> This feature isn’t available to everyone. You can only share a reel to Facebook from the Instagram app for Android or iPhone. <div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Before you begin</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">When you create a reel on Instagram, you can share it to your Facebook account. You can only share a reel when you create it.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Share your reel to Facebook</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Note:</b> To share your reels to Facebook, your Instagram and Facebook accounts need to be added to the same <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/1731078377046291" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_36_bg"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">Accounts Center</span></span></a>.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Share all future reels to Facebook automatically</b></h5><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Turn off automatic crossposting to a Facebook Page</b></h5><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">To turn off automatic crossposting to a Facebook page:</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Go to your profile and tap <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=fb-sharingmenuhamburger-everyphone&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b>Menu</b> in the top right.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Tap <b>Crossposting</b>.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">In the crossposting settings, tap <b>Accounts Center</b>.</p></li><li><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Under <b>Automatically share</b>, tap the toggle <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=messenger-toggle-iphone&amp;density=1" alt="" /> next to the type of posts you no longer want to crosspost. Select the types of posts you no longer want to crosspost:</p><ul class="uiList _4of _4kg"><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Your Instagram posts</b></p></div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Your Instagram story</b></p></div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Your Instagram reels</b></p></div></li></ul></li></ol><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>What happens when you share your reels to Facebook</b></h5><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">When you share your Instagram reels to Facebook, they’ll appear as reels on Facebook.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If you haven’t selected a <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://www.facebook.com/help/213594365329782" target="_blank" id="u_0_37_mF"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">default audience for reels on Facebook</span></span></a>, your default audience for reels on Facebook will be based on your Instagram account privacy setting:</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ul class="uiList _4of _4kg"><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Public Instagram account</b>: Your Facebook Reels audience will default to public.</p></div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Private Instagram account</b>: Your Facebook Reels audience will default to friends on Facebook.</p></div></li></ul><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If you are under 18, your Facebook Reels audience will default to friends on Facebook.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Keep in mind that your followers on Instagram may not be the same as your friends on Facebook, so if you share a reel from Instagram to Facebook, you may be sharing your reel with different people. You can <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://www.facebook.com/help/204305118221546" target="_blank" id="u_0_38_ef"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">change your default audience</span></span></a> for reels on Facebook at any time. Reels can appear in feed, Watch and other places across Facebook.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If your reels are shared to public on Facebook, people on Facebook will be able to use the original audio from your reel. If your default audience for reels on Facebook is public and you have remixing enabled on Instagram, they’ll also be able to remix your reel. You can <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/792438641600810" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_39_VP"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">change your remix setting</span></span></a> in the Instagram app at any time.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/1392551434468488" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3a_Zi"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">Who can see your reel</span></span></a> on Instagram follows your account’s privacy settings.</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If you post a reel on Instagram and your account is public, you can choose to have your reel shown as <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/2335700983566418" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3b_GL"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">recommended content on Facebook</span></span></a> instead.</p><h5 class="_a8td _abs3" style=""><b>Troubleshooting</b></h5><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">If you&#039;re having trouble sharing your reel to Facebook, try some of <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/1206864193634431" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3c_4n"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">these troubleshooting steps</span></span></a>.</p></div></div></div></div><div style="border-color:black" class="_8xfk _8xfl"><button data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-7&quot;&#125;" class="_8xfm" id="u_0_3d_gB"><div class="_8xfn"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs2" style="">Manage reels on Instagram</p></div></div></div><img class="_9csc img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-7-expand&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yM/r/LzRvQbnX_o1.svg" alt="" id="u_0_3e_h5" /><img class="_9csb hidden_elem img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-7-collapse&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yA/r/CwHyQHI-5OE.svg" alt="" id="u_0_3f_sm" /></button><div class="_8xfq hidden_elem" id="u_0_3g_it"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><div class="mvm uiP fsm"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">After posting a reel, you can share, save, manage or delete your reel.</p><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Share and save your reel</b></p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Manage your reel</b></p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="mvm uiP fsm"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Other Reels settings</b></p><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">You can also manage other settings for your reels including:</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ul class="uiList _4of _4kg"><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Who can <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/1743992069496286/?helpref=platform_switcher&amp;cms_platform=iphone-app&amp;cms_id=1743992069496286" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3h_FY"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw"><u>repost your reel</u></span></span></a>.</p></div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Who can <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/792438641600810" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3i_G5"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">remix your reel</span></span></a>.</p></div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Using <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/700284123459336" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3j_+7"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">comment filters</span></span></a> to determine what comments are allowed on your reels.</p></div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/289302621183285" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3k_be"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">Delete your reel</span></span></a> from Instagram.</p></div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Who can mention you in the caption of their reel by changing <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" class="_95iu _8iqw">your account&#039;s mention settings</span></span></a>. You’ll receive a notification when someone mentions you in a reel. Learn more about <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/627963287377328" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3m_wt"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">how mentions work on Instagram</span></span></a>.</p></div></li><li><div class="fcb"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">Whether to <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/2335700983566418" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3n_47"><span style="-webkit-background-clip: text; -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;" class="_8-vl _8-vp"><span class="_95iu _8iqw">recommend your reel on Facebook</span></span></a>.</p></div></li></ul></div></div></div></div><div style="border-color:black" class="_8xfk _8xfl"><button data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-8&quot;&#125;" class="_8xfm" id="u_0_3o_Cf"><div class="_8xfn"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs2" style="">See info about your reel on Instagram</p></div></div></div><img class="_9csc img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-8-expand&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yM/r/LzRvQbnX_o1.svg" alt="" id="u_0_3p_BU" /><img class="_9csb hidden_elem img" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative_detail&quot;:&quot;reels_FAQ-question-8-collapse&quot;&#125;" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/rsrc.php/yA/r/CwHyQHI-5OE.svg" alt="" id="u_0_3q_G4" /></button><div class="_8xfq hidden_elem" id="u_0_3r_zv"><div class="_8g8k"><div class="_8g8k _8g8n _8g8_"><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">See how many people liked, commented on, and reshared your Instagram reel. Deleting a comment on a shared reel removes it from both Instagram and Facebook.</p><p class="_a88i _abs3" style="">To see info about your reel:</p><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><ol><li><p class="_3p8">Click your profile picture on the left to go to your profile.</p></li><li>Click <img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=ig-reels-shared&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Reels</b> below your profile info. In the bottom left of each reel, you’ll see the number of video plays (how many times your reel starts to play). This doesn&#039;t include replays.</li><li>Hover over the reel you want to see more info for. From here, you’ll see a number below indicating the insights.</li></ol><ul class="uiList _4of _4kg"><li><div class="fcb"><img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=instagram-heartlikedposts-ios&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Likes</b> that shows how many people liked your reel.</div></li><li><div class="fcb"><img class="img" src="https://static.xx.fbcdn.net/assets/?set=content_operations_help_center&amp;name=instagram-comment&amp;density=1" alt="" /><b> Comments</b> that shows how many people commented on your reel. Click the reel to see the comments.</div></li></ul><div class="_8irf _8irh _8irp"></div><p class="_a88i _abs3" style=""><b>Note:</b> If you recommend your reel to Facebook, play and like counts may include <a class="_8ow9" data-ms="&#123;&quot;creative&quot;:&quot;click_external-link&quot;&#125;" href="https://help.instagram.com/2335700983566418" data-lnfb-mode="ie" id="u_0_3s_8L"><span interactions</span></span></a>.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="_8iwr" id="u_0_3t_4Z"><div class="_8g8k _8g8p _8g8t _8g91 _8g95"><div class="_8g87 _8g89 _8g5_ _8hkx _8hk-"><p class="_a8td _a6sv _a4km _a4kh" style="">Download for iOS/Android.</p><div class="_8irf _8irj _8irr"></div><div style="display: flex; flex-direction: row;"><p class="_a8td _abs3" style="margin:0px 0px
